i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0;               double* __restrict__ out) {\n    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&\n                      (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0,\n values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; A group of","+//   THREADS_PER_ROW consecutive threads (\"sub-warp\") cooperates on a single row:","+//   the group strides through the row's non-zeros, which yields fully coalesced","+//   accesses to val[] and cols[], and the per-thread partial sums are reduced","+//   with warp shuffles. The number of threads per row is chosen at launch time","+//   to match the average number of non-zeros per row.","+//","+//   Rows are processed with a grid-stride loop so the launch configuration is","+//   independent of the matrix size.","+//","+// Arguments:","+//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix","+//   cols: array of column indices for each element","+//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows","+//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication","+//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix","+//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation","+//","+// ****************************************************************************","+template <int THREADS_PER_ROW>","+__global__ void spmvCsrVector(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,","+                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,","+                              const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,","+                              double* __restrict__ out) {","+    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&","+                      (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0,","+                  \"THREADS_PER_ROW must be a power of two in [1, 32]\");","+","+    const unsigned int tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;","+    const unsigned int lane = tid & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1); offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {","+            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);","+        }","+","+        if (lane == 0) {","+            out[row] = t;","+        }","+    }","+}","+","+// ****************************************************************************","+// Function: launchSpmv","+//","+// Purpose:","+//   Launches the CSR SpMV kernel with a number of threads per row matching the","+//   average row length, and a grid sized to fill the device.","+//","+// ****************************************************************************","+void launchSpmv(const int threadsPerRow, const int numBlocks, const double* d_val,","+                const index_t* d_cols, const index_t* d_rowDelimiters, const double* d_vec,","+                const index_t dim, double* d_out) {","+    switch (threadsPerRow) {","+        case 1:","+            spmvCsrVector<1><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        case 2:","+            spmvCsrVector<2><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        case 4:","+            spmvCsrVector<4><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        case 8:","+            spmvCsrVector<8><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        case 16:","+            spmvCsrVector<16><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+        default:","+            spmvCsrVector<32><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);","+            break;","+    }","+}","+","+// ****************************************************************************"," // Function: verifyResults"," //"," // Purpose:"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvCsrVector\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CUDA implementation of CSR sparse matrix-vector multiplication. A group of\n//   THREADS_PER_ROW consecutive threads (\"sub-warp\") cooperates on a single row:\n//   the group strides through the row's non-zeros, which yields fully coalesced\n//   accesses to val[] and cols[], and the per-thread partial sums are reduced\n//   with warp shuffles. The number of threads per row is chosen at launch time\n//   to match the average number of non-zeros per row.\n//\n//   Rows are processed with a grid-stride loop so the launch configuration is\n//   independent of the matrix size.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int THREADS_PER_ROW>\n__global__ void spmvCsrVector(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,\n                              double* __restrict__ out) {\n    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&\n                      (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0,\n                  \"THREADS_PER_ROW must be a power of two in [1, 32]\");\n\n    const unsigned int tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned int lane = tid & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);  // lane within the row group\n    const unsigned int numGroups = gridDim.x * blockDim.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;\n\n    for (index_t row = tid / THREADS_PER_ROW; row < dim; row += numGroups) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += THREADS_PER_ROW) THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: launchSpmv\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Launches the CSR SpMV kernel with a number of threads per row matching the\n//   average row length, and a grid sized to fill the device.\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid launchSpmv(const int BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvCsrVector<8><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvCsrVector<16><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        default:\n if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                return false;\n            }\n                      \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvCsrVector\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CUDA implementation of CSR sparse matrix-vector multiplication. A group of\n//   THREADS_PER_ROW consecutive threads (\"sub-warp\") cooperates on a single row:\n//   the group strides through the row's non-zeros, which yields fully coalesced\n//   accesses to val[] and cols[], and the per-thread partial sums are reduced\n//   with warp shuffles. The number of threads per row is chosen at launch time\n//   to match the average number of non-zeros per row.\n//\n//   Rows are processed with a grid-stride loop so the launch configuration is\n//   independent of the matrix size.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int THREADS_PER_ROW>\n__global__ void spmvCsrVector(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,\n                              double* __restrict__ out) {\n    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&\n                      (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0,\n                  \"THREADS_PER_ROW must be a power of two in [1, 32]\");\n\n    const unsigned int tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned int lane = tid & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);  // lane within the row group\n    const unsigned int numGroups = gridDim.x * blockDim.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;\n\n    // Shuffle mask covering exactly this thread's row group: groups of a warp may\n    // execute a different number of grid-stride iterations, so they must not be\n    // named as participants of each other's reductions.\n    const unsigned int groupMask =\n        (THREADS_PER_ROW == 32)\n            ? 0xffffffffu\n            : (((1u << THREADS_PER_ROW) - 1u) << ((threadIdx.x & 31u) & ~(THREADS_PER_ROW - 1u)));\n\n    for (index_t row = tid / THREADS_PER_ROW; row < dim; row += numGroups) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += THREADS_PER_ROW) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n        // Reduce the partial sums held by the THREADS_PER_ROW threads of this group\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(groupMask, t, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: launchSpmv\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Launches the CSR SpMV kernel with a number of The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Kernel: spmvCsrVector\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   CUDA implementation of CSR sparse matrix-vector multiplication. A group of\n//   THREADS_PER_ROW consecutive threads (\"sub-warp\") cooperates on a single row:\n//   the group strides through the row's non-zeros, which yields fully coalesced\n//   accesses to val[] and cols[], and the per-thread partial sums are reduced\n//   with warp shuffles. The number of threads per row is chosen at launch time\n//   to match the average number of non-zeros per row.\n//\n//   Rows are processed with a grid-stride loop so the launch configuration is\n//   independent of the matrix size.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int THREADS_PER_ROW>\n__global__ void spmvCsrVector(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                              const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                              const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t dim,\n                              double* __restrict__ out) {\n    static_assert(THREADS_PER_ROW >= 1 && THREADS_PER_ROW <= 32 &&\n                      (THREADS_PER_ROW & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1)) == 0,\n                  \"THREADS_PER_ROW must be a power of two in [1, 32]\");\n\n    const unsigned int tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned int lane = tid & (THREADS_PER_ROW - 1);  // lane within the row group\n    const unsigned int numGroups = gridDim.x * blockDim.x / THREADS_PER_ROW;\n\n    // Shuffle mask covering exactly this thread's row group: groups of a warp may\n    // execute a different number of grid-stride iterations, so they must not be\n    // named as participants of each other's reductions.\n    const unsigned int groupMask =\n        (THREADS_PER_ROW == 32)\n            ? 0xffffffffu\n            : (((1u << THREADS_PER_ROW) - 1u) << ((threadIdx.x & 31u) & ~(THREADS_PER_ROW - 1u)));\n\n    for (index_t row = tid / THREADS_PER_ROW; row < dim; row += numGroups) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += THREADS_PER_ROW) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n        // Reduce the partial sums held by the THREADS_PER_ROW threads of this group\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = THREADS_PER_ROW / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(groupMask, t, offset, THREADS_PER_ROW);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvCsrVector<8><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvCsrVector<16><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvCsrVector<32><<<numBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_val, d_cols, d_rowDelimiters, d_vec, dim, d_out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; The benchmark now runs SpMV unconditionally on the GPU.\n\n**What changed** (only `spmv/spmv.cpp` and `spmv/CMakeLists.txt`; executable still `spmv`):\n\n- **`spmvCsrVector<THREADS_PER_ROW>` kernel** (`spmv/spmv.cpp:105`): CSR \"vector\" kernel where a group of `THREADS_PER_ROW` consecutive threads cooperates on one row, striding through its non-zeros so `val[]`/`cols[]` accesses are coalesced, then reducing partials with `__shfl_down_sync`. The dense vector is gathered through the read-only cache (`__ldg`). Rows are covered by a grid-stride loop, so the launch config is independent of matrix size.\n- **Shuffle mask**: groups within a warp can execute a different number of grid-stride iterations, so each group reduces with a mask covering exactly its own lanes rather than `0xffffffff` (which would name non-participating threads — UB on Volta+).\n- **Adaptive threads-per-row**: chosen at runtime as the power of two ≥ average nnz/row, clamped to a warp (1/2/4/8/16/32), so both very sparse and dense rows stay efficient. Grid is sized to cover all rows but capped at 32 blocks/SM.\n- **Host side**: device buffers allocated and matrix/vector uploaded once before timing; a warm-up launch precedes the timed loop; the timed region covers the iteration loop, the device sync, and the result copy-back. All CUDA calls go through a checking macro.\n- **Metrics**: the `Computation time: N ms` line is unchanged, but GFLOPS/avg-time are now derived from a microsecond-resolution measurement — at GPU speeds the millisecond count is often 0, which would have produced `inf`.\n- **CMakeLists**: `LANGUAGES CXX CUDA`, `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, and `set_source_files_properties(spmv.cpp PROPERTIES LANGUAGE CUDA)` so the existing file compiles with nvcc without renaming it. No new dependencies.\n\n`spmvCpu` is retained unchanged as the `-v` reference.\n\n**Verification** (RTX 3090, sm_86):\n- `-v` passes across sizes and sparsities, including every threads-per-row branch (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32) and non-power-of-two row counts.\n- The grid-stride wrap path with sub-warp groups was exercised by temporarily forcing a 3-block grid in a throwaway `/tmp` copy — all configurations validated.\n- Performance at `-n 16384 -s 100`: **87 GFLOPS** vs **1.42 GFLOPS** for the original serial loop measured on the same input (~61x); `-n 32768 -s 200` reaches 104 GFLOPS, near the memory-bandwidth roof for this access pattern.\n\nOne semantic note: summation within a row is reassociated across threads, so results differ from the CPU in the last bits. That is inherent to any parallel reduction; the 2% validator passes, but the `-r` hash will not match a serial run bit-for-bit (Sum/Min/Max/Samples agree to ~1e-15 relative)."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":656,"cache_read_input_tokens":37971,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":656},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d138406b-de6c-4da8-9460-70a6e1d5a0cd","uuid":"59ed2006-7ed6-41d7-8608-e0daeb4cfb32","timestamp":"2026-09-05T20:26:37.388Z","request_id":"req_011CekvNP5NKNiTpCBhY8e88"}
